**[00:17:15.620] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000136)

**[00:18:51.520] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000149)

**[00:19:12.340] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000151)

na maayos at maibalik po ang tiwala ng taong bayan sa ating gobyerno.

**[00:22:27.730] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000195)  
Sang-ayon po ako sa ating Chairman na nasa crossroads ho tayo

**[00:22:31.850] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000196)  
pagdating sa tiwala.

**[00:22:32.770] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000197)  
Hindi na ho

**[00:22:34.770] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000198)  
Hindi na ho tinitingnan ng taong bayan

**[00:22:37.770] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000199)  
kung ikaw ay congressman, senator, or judge, or justice, or mayor.

**[00:22:43.770] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000200)  
Tingin nila, buong gobyerno ay nawawala na po ang tiwala nila.

**[00:22:48.770] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000201)  
Kaya nandito ho kami para mabigyan po ng suporta ang judiciary

**[00:22:55.770] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000202)  
para po 'yung wheels of justice, 'yung gulong ng hustisya,

**[00:23:00.770] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000203)  
ay gumana

**[00:23:01.770] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000204)  
at maging episyente.

**[00:23:02.770] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000205)  
Marami ho talaga tayong dapat ayusin

**[00:23:04.770] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000206)  
at marami ho tayong dapat panagutin,

**[00:23:09.770] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000207)  
lalo na dito po sa mga nangyaring corruption issues.

**[00:23:13.770] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000208)  
At hindi ho magiging kumpleto po itong pagbabalik ng tiwala

**[00:23:19.770] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000209)  
kung isa sa ating mga sangay ng ating pamahalaan ay hindi po gagana.

**[00:23:25.770] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000210)  
Kaya nandito ho kami para suportahan ho kayo.

**[00:23:30.770] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000211)  
Pero importante rin po na ang hanay natin ay masigurado natin ay gumagana at lahat po ay sumusuporta sa parehong adikain na maibalik po ang tiwala ng taong bayan sa ating gobyerno.

**[00:23:48.110] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000212)  
So with that, Chairman, maraming maraming salamat po.

**[00:23:50.370] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000213)

**[00:31:51.170] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000287)  
For our major final outputs, Your Honor,

**[00:31:55.470] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000288)  
or case disposition, the following slides

**[00:31:57.870] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000289)  
represent disposition from the Supreme Court down to our first-level courts.

**[00:32:04.310] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000290)  
From the data, many of our courts are already exceeding their disposal targets with accomplishment rates reaching 85% to 290%.

**[00:32:14.110] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000291)  
With our first-level courts being our champions in case disposition, considering the volume of cases incoming and the 24-hour limit in the disposal of cases with the application of the rules and expedited procedures in the first-level courts.

**[00:32:28.390] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000292)  
The figures, however, Your Honor, only represent productivity and not the entirety of the performance.

**[00:32:35.050] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000293)  
Because under the SPJI, the Supreme Court has begun to pilot the high-performance courts framework.

**[00:32:41.370] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000294)  
Instead of just the number, it looks at internal processes, decision quality, court user satisfaction, and employee engagement.

**[00:32:49.390] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000295)  
In that sense, the high-performance court, Your Honors, is the next evolution of our performance measurement from volume of output to decrement.

**[00:32:58.390] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000296)  
Quality and overall effectiveness of justice delivery.

**[00:33:01.910] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000297)  
So we are moving from measuring how much a court does to understanding how well a court delivers justice.

**[00:33:09.550] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000298)  
Your Honors, also another important component of our effort to improve court performance, which will affect case disposition, is the continuing professionalization and decentralization of court administration through the Office of the Regional Court Managers, or ORCMs.

**[00:33:26.890] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000299)  
The objective is to...

**[00:33:28.390] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000300)  
Allow our courts to focus on their core mandate in the adjudication and disposition of cases, while administrative and operational requirements are increasingly supported through a dedicated court management structure.

**[00:33:41.670] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000301)  
So this directly supports our high-performance courts initiative.

**[00:33:45.370] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000302)  
A high-performance court should not only dispose of cases efficiently.

**[00:33:50.510] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000303)  
It should have the administrative support systems that enable judges and court personnel to devote more of their time and attention to their primary...

**[00:33:58.390] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000304)  
judicial functions.

**[00:33:59.390] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000305)  
Now, for our SPJI reforms, Your Honor, we likewise listed our milestones for our... for 2026.

**[00:34:07.390] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000306)  
Your Honors, we have always invoked that the SPJI is our roadmap for transforming the judiciary, not only through technology, but through improvements in the way courts are managed, cases are processed, resources are administered, and services are delivered to the public.

**[00:34:25.390] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000307)  
What we present in these slides are milestones of the SPJI reform.

**[00:34:27.590] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000308)  
Thank you.

**[00:34:28.110] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000309)  
And now we would like to thank the Office of the Regional Court Managers for their holistic participation in this program.

**[00:34:30.930] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000310)  
They demonstrate that the judiciary has already moved from planning to actual implementation across a broad range of areas.

**[00:34:39.150] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000311)  
These initiatives include the eCourt PH, the eCourt, and the Philippine Judiciary Platform, which support the digitalization of court processes.

**[00:34:48.710] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000312)  
The AI system of voice-to-text transcription, which is listed as number one, which can assist in the preparation of transcripts and court records.

**[00:34:56.290] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000313)  
We likewise have the development...

**[00:34:58.110] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000314)  
of the ICT governance framework, the ICT strategic plan, cloud infrastructure, connectivity and data

**[00:35:05.370] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000315)  
center capabilities which provide the technological backbone for these reforms. We are likewise

**[00:35:11.550] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000316)  
building systems for financial management, human resource management, procurement, electronic

**[00:35:17.130] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000317)  
payments, online learning and case and court facility management. These are important because

**[00:35:22.730] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000318)  
modernization is not confined to what happens inside the courtroom because it also involves

**[00:35:28.710] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000319)  
modernizing the administrative systems that support the courts. We are also developing

**[00:35:34.170] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000320)  
initiatives that directly improve access to the justice system including the unified legal aid

**[00:35:40.090] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000321)  
service portal, e-notarization and other online services for the bar and other justice sectors

**[00:35:46.390] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000322)  
stakeholders. The significance of these initiatives, your honors, is that they are designed to cascade

**[00:35:52.210] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000323)  
beyond the court system. We are also developing initiatives that directly improve access to the

**[00:35:52.710] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000324)  
Supreme Court and benefit the courts throughout the country. The objective is not to create

**[00:35:58.570] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000325)  
isolated technological solutions but to build an integrated judiciary in which information,

**[00:36:05.170] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000326)  
systems, processes and resources can support courts at every level. For our JSCC, your honors,

**[00:36:14.210] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000327)  
the flagship program of the JSCC or the Justice Sector Coordinating Council under the Justice

**[00:36:19.590] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000328)  
Convergence Program is the Justice Zone. At the local level, the JSCC is the justice zone.

**[00:36:23.970] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000329)  
At the local level, the different pillars of the criminal justice system come together

**[00:36:27.130] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000330)  
to identify the actual causes of delay and inefficiency and develop coordinated

**[00:36:32.410] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000331)  
locally responsive solutions despite the difference in our mandate. For now, your honor,

**[00:36:38.230] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000332)  
we have developed 20 justice systems. We have Zambanga City, which we have declared the

**[00:36:43.610] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000333)  
trafficking-free justice zone. We have Tagaytay City Justice Zone, which we have declared the

**[00:36:48.130] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000334)  
economic development and tourism justice zone. We have Puerto Princesa or the green justice zone.

**[00:36:53.970] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000335)  
We have likewise Iligan, Ozamis, Cagayan de Oro, Tacloban or Moc and Calbayog as our anti-OSIAC

**[00:37:01.170] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000336)  
justice zones or the online sexual exploitation of children justice zones. Another important

**[00:37:08.010] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000337)  
initiative of the judiciary, your honors, is the establishment and strengthening of the Office of

**[00:37:12.890] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000338)  
the Judiciary Marshals pursuant to Republic Act 1169 or the Judiciary Marshals Act. The funding

**[00:37:19.110] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000339)  
supports the development of a professional judiciary marshal organization with capability,

**[00:37:23.970] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000340)  
judicial security and operations, investigation and intelligence, legal services and digital

**[00:37:30.370] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000341)  
forensics. Its function includes threat assessment, investigation, forensic analysis,

**[00:37:36.290] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000342)  
protection of court facilities and personnel, even their members of the families,

**[00:37:40.510] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000343)  
and ensuring that court proceedings are conducted in a safe and orderly environment. In our 2026

**[00:37:46.750] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000344)  
GAIA, your honor, we were only allowed a budget of 710 million and we still need to fill in the

**[00:37:53.250] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000345)  
vacancies of the plantilla, Your Honor.

**[00:37:53.970] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000346)  
So we are proposing a budget

**[00:37:54.110] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000347)  
plantilia, your honor. So we are proposing a budget of 902 million 75 thousand pesos or an

**[00:38:01.930] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000348)  
increase of 25, 27 percent. For the Philippine bar as an investment in the bar and in the legal

**[00:38:09.450] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000349)  
profession and to improve the quality, efficiency, accessibility and integrity in the admission of

**[00:38:14.850] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000350)  
the profession, the Supreme Court holds the annual bar exams and the sharia special bar exams.

**[00:38:20.750] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-000351)  
We are midstream now, your honor, in the current,

**[00:46:27.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000446)  
Good morning to our Vice Chair, Senator Francis Pangilinan. Of course, to our respected distinguished

**[00:46:32.840] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000447)  
associate justices of the Supreme Court, presiding justices and associate justices of our appellate

**[00:46:39.440] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000448)  
and specialized courts, court administrators, colleagues in the Senate, and all our esteemed

**[00:46:44.060] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000449)  
guests present today, we fully recognize and uphold the judiciary's vital role as an independent

**[00:46:49.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000450)  
co-equal branch of government. As such, for the judiciary to remain fully, truly independent and

**[00:46:56.060] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000451)  
perform its constitutional mandate.

**[00:46:57.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000452)  
It

**[00:46:57.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000453)  
must have sufficient control over its resources. This is why we supported the

**[00:47:03.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000454)  
enactment of Republic Act No. 12233 or the Judiciary Fiscal Autonomy Act. The proposed budget of

**[00:47:10.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000455)  
the judiciary for the fiscal year 2027 marks the very first year of the implementation of this law.

**[00:47:19.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000456)  
While we advocate the judicial independence, our basic principle is still we want to ensure that

**[00:47:25.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000457)  
every peso invested in our justice system directly reduces trial bottlenecks,

**[00:47:27.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000458)  
modernizes our courthouses and delivers swift, accessible justice to every Filipino. I look forward to a very productive discussion today, Mr. Chair. We are very much in full support of the judiciary.

**[00:47:55.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000459)

**[01:14:17.220] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001009)

**[01:14:34.560] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001013)

**[01:14:56.440] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001022)

**[01:15:00.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001023)

**[01:15:40.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001029)

**[01:15:47.580] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001030)

**[01:15:50.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001031)

**[01:15:56.440] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001032)

**[01:18:27.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001085)

**[01:18:29.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001086)  
Just like what I have, I received a letter from the DBM.

**[01:18:38.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001087)  
The first copy of the comment on your proposed 2037 budget, section 3 of RA 12233.

**[01:18:45.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001088)  
Here it is.

**[01:18:45.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001089)  
They're recommending a budget ceiling of 77.

**[01:18:47.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001090)  
77 billion, which leaves 9 billion, 9.17 billion gap from the judiciaries.

**[01:18:54.580] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001091)  
Your proposal is 86 billion, 86.37 billion.

**[01:18:59.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001092)  
So, of course, the basic questions I would like to ask, which specific program or projects

**[01:19:04.700] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001093)  
will strongly be affected of this 9.17 billion difference?

**[01:19:10.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001094)  
Maybe the DBM, because of the judicial...

**[01:19:16.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001095)  
Autonomy.

**[01:19:17.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001096)  
Maybe this is the last chance for them to comment or to intervene.

**[01:19:23.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001097)  
You know, the DBM, if they can reduce it, they will reduce it.

**[01:19:26.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001098)  
But because of the low fiscal ceiling also this year.

**[01:19:30.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001099)  
So, specifically, what are the major programs and projects that will be affected by the

**[01:19:37.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001100)  
9.17 billion difference?

**[01:19:40.740] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001101)  
Your Honor, because we're really banking on three factors, Your Honor.

**[01:19:44.700] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001102)  
Our people, system, and infrastructure.

**[01:19:47.820] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001103)  
So, if we decrease it by 9 billion, definitely in our people, our plans to upgrade, reclassify

**[01:19:55.460] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001104)  
the salaries of our people will be affected.

**[01:20:00.980] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001105)  
And most of our personnel, Your Honor, have not had the salary increase.

**[01:20:05.020] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001106)  
And because there's no really point of them being promoted, because there's no such thing

**[01:20:09.940] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001107)  
in the courts, Your Honor.

**[01:20:10.940] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001108)  
If you come in as a utility, the only chance that you can become as that salary grade one,

**[01:20:16.860] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001109)  
that the only chance...

**[01:20:17.820] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001110)  
The only chance that you can be promoted as a process server, considering the qualification

**[01:20:21.520] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001111)  
standards that you can, which is SG2, will be second to none.

**[01:20:25.780] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001112)  
Because most of the time, we are really awaiting for the retirement of most members of our

**[01:20:30.900] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001113)  
people.

**[01:20:31.760] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001114)  
So, if we decrease the infrastructure, can you imagine, Your Honor?

**[01:20:36.100] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001115)  
I mean, I cannot even say that our courts are beyond dignified in the image.

**[01:20:41.920] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001116)  
We really have to do a lot of...

**[01:20:43.680] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001117)  
Especially, Your Honors, the fact that most of our buildings are 20 years and older.

**[01:20:47.820] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001118)  
With the calamities that have been happening, we've been going around, our buildings are

**[01:20:52.520] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001119)  
old and do need...

**[01:20:54.440] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001120)  
They need rehabilitation, repairs, and a lot of, if not, demolition, Your Honor.

**[01:21:00.680] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001121)  
HOJ in Iloilo, Your Honor, I'm not even sure, but it has been vacated.

**[01:21:05.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001122)  
It has been condemned.

**[01:21:07.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001123)  
It was retrofitted.

**[01:21:09.820] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001124)  
Yes, but most of our judges do not feel safe anymore to go back, which is why we had to

**[01:21:15.020] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001125)  
relocate them to another one, to another building.

**[01:21:17.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001126)  
I don't know if it's coincidence, but in Iloilo, when there's a flyover, there's a lot of

**[01:21:23.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001127)  
people.

**[01:21:24.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001128)  
Then, the Hall of Justice, that's new.

**[01:21:27.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001129)  
Then, it's already unfit for occupancy.

**[01:21:33.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001130)  
There's a need to retrofit again.

**[01:21:35.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001131)  
The flyover that was like a rollercoaster, it was retrofitted.

**[01:21:39.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001132)  
It's new.

**[01:21:39.840] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001133)  
Now, it's being retrofitted again.

**[01:21:41.840] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001134)  
Mr. Chair, I would like to...

**[01:21:43.060] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001135)  
What's the name of the DBM?

**[01:21:45.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001136)  
Oh, yeah, please.

**[01:21:46.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001137)  
With your indulgence.

**[01:21:46.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001138)  
With your indulgence.

**[01:21:47.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001139)  
So, some things, the Manila Hall of Justice proposal, what are the causes of delays?

**[01:21:57.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001140)  
I'd just like to ask that the Hall of Justice in Manila is again delayed because the 1.918

**[01:22:07.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001141)  
billion second year funding, supposedly, for the Manila Hall of Justice.

**[01:22:12.480] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001142)  
So, what are the causes of delays?

**[01:22:15.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001143)

**[01:22:18.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001144)  
That's what I mentioned earlier.

**[01:22:20.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001145)  
So, what are the bottlenecks?

**[01:22:23.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001146)  
And I think this is more than 26 years overdue now, or 20...

**[01:22:28.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001147)  
That's right, 30 years already.

**[01:22:32.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001148)  
Justice Lopez said, 1992, when the first groundbreaking took place.

**[01:22:38.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001149)  
So, go ahead, ma'am.

**[01:22:39.860] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001150)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:22:41.280] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001151)  
Mr. Chair, the Department of Budget and Management has already...

**[01:22:43.280] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001152)  
The Department of Budget and Management has already...

**[01:22:44.160] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001153)  
The Department of Budget and Management has already...

**[01:22:45.160] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001154)

**[01:22:50.000] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001155)  
a copy of the same.

**[01:22:50.860] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001156)  
So, we have not even read it.

**[01:22:52.460] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001157)  
But to answer your honor's question, your honor, on what caused the delay in the Manila

**[01:22:57.400] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001158)  
Hall of Justice project, your honor, I do know that there was already an awarded contractor

**[01:23:04.120] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001159)  
way back in, before I...

**[01:23:07.340] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001160)  
I was still an art regional trial court judge, your honor, but it was complicated by a technical

**[01:23:12.900] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001161)  
mathematical computation, your honor.

**[01:23:15.140] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001162)  
Which way, the honorable Supreme Court had to terminate the contractor, the works that

**[01:23:24.860] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001163)  
was already procured because of that error.

**[01:23:32.480] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001164)  
So, the diet consultant was terminated, and then we had to repost it again.

**[01:23:37.080] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001165)  
We had two failed biddings.

**[01:23:38.500] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001166)  
And then now, it was only the second semester of 2024 that a consulting certifying...

**[01:23:45.140] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001167)  
was engaged and is about to submit the DAED, Your Honor.

**[01:23:50.260] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001168)  
So it is not an ordinary construction project, Your Honor.

**[01:23:54.680] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001169)  
So we really have to make it ready, especially make it disaster resilient

**[01:23:59.060] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001170)  
with all the natural calamities that we are experiencing.

**[01:24:02.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001171)  
So may pag-asa pa naman na before matapos ang term is NKCO ulit.

**[01:24:05.980] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001172)

**[01:24:06.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001173)

**[01:24:11.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001174)

**[01:45:00.340] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001680)

**[01:45:08.040] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001682)

**[01:45:17.500] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001683)

**[01:45:23.660] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001684)

**[01:45:28.460] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001685)

**[01:45:42.400] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001686)

**[01:46:08.040] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001693)

**[01:46:08.700] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001694)  
Outside extraneous…

**[01:46:10.260] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001695)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[01:46:11.100] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001696)

**[01:46:14.440] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001697)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[01:46:15.480] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001698)  
That's why the Sandigan Bayan is actually pushing for speedy disposition of cases, especially the cases that are recently filed.

**[01:50:01.680] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001736)

**[01:50:16.180] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001739)

**[01:50:22.210] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001740)

**[01:50:31.870] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001741)  
There's this…

**[01:50:32.590] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001742)

And in 2025, bumagsak tayo to rank 120.

**[01:50:53.530] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001748)  
We're only higher than Myanmar and Cambodia, which are military junta regimes.

**[01:51:01.190] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001749)  
So, in other words…

**[01:51:03.630] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001750)  
The perception of our international peers, mga kasamahan na natin sa ibang bansa, is lumalalaing corruption sa atin.

**[01:51:16.390] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001751)  
And that also is connected to investments in our country.

**[01:51:22.090] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001752)  
That's why I saw numbers in our investments.

**[01:51:24.250] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001753)  
It's also gone down.

**[01:51:27.390] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001754)  
Lately, the Business Confidence Index by local businessmen has plunged to a negative.

**[01:51:32.850] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001755)  
Territory, which is actually even worse than the pandemic time.

**[01:51:38.330] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001756)  
So, in other words, the perception of corruption, and also government addressing it, is quite low.

**[01:51:45.650] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001757)  
So, I just want to ask the Sandigan, how are we also addressing this perception of corruption?

**[01:51:54.750] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001758)  
As well as, how is the Sandigan also working with the other branches of government?

**[01:52:02.690] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001759)  
To improve our perception of corruption, and eventually make people accountable.

**[01:52:09.050] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001760)  
At the end of the day, ang gusto naman natin kung ang evident siya, claro, then let's make people accountable.

**[01:52:14.930] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001761)  
So, and like I said, the executive, legislative, and judiciary, in the eyes of our kababayan, isa lang yan eh.

**[01:52:22.850] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001762)  
One government lang.

**[01:52:24.210] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001763)  
So, we want to ask, and right now is a crucial time because of the corruption issue.

**[01:52:28.770] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001764)  
So, I want to ask the Sandigan.

**[01:52:31.170] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001765)  
How are we?

**[01:52:32.690] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001766)  
In your internal management discussion, what are the mechanisms that you are employing to improve the perception of corruption in our country?

**[01:52:49.310] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001767)  
Thank you, Mr. Senate President.

**[01:52:50.890] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001768)  
Actually, this Corruption Perception Index is a report of Transparency International, an NGO, an international NGO.

**[01:53:02.130] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001769)  
And there are several parameters.

**[01:53:04.010] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001770)  
That are being considered in rating a country in terms of its performance vis-a-vis corruption.

**[01:53:14.990] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001771)  
The judiciary certainly plays a role.

**[01:53:19.090] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001772)  
But it is only a specific portion that it can address.

**[01:53:25.810] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001773)  
And that is in the speedy disposition of cases, one.

**[01:53:31.770] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001774)  
Two, that the justices.

**[01:53:34.010] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001775)  
And the court system must appear to be credible that it is dispensing justice.

**[01:53:42.590] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001776)  
Important is this credibility of the justice system and the judges, justices, that we can be fair in the decisions that we give.

**[01:53:56.230] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001777)  
And I think the Supreme Court is stressing the need for the justices and the judges to be credible.

**[01:54:05.030] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001780)  
It is very strong now, the Supreme Court, especially under the Chief Justice, SPJI.

**[01:54:12.310] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001781)  
That the judges should also be ethical.

**[01:54:15.790] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001782)  
And that we maintain our values as citizens and as judges.

**[01:54:24.550] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001783)  
Once we are credible enough.

**[01:54:27.090] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001784)  
Once the people will have faith and the Filipino citizen will have faith that we can deliver.

**[01:54:34.010] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001785)  
and give that justice to the Filipino citizens,

**[01:54:39.230] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001786)  
then perception of justice and non-corruption will increase.

**[01:54:47.030] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001787)  
The step forward for us in the Sandigan Bayan is to ensure that when cases are filed,

**[01:54:55.110] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001788)  
we attend to the cases and that they are heard immediately.

**[01:54:59.810] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001789)  
Thus, right now in the Sandigan Bayan, everybody, it is open in the internet.

**[01:55:08.790] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001790)  
You can see where the cases are ruffled to.

**[01:55:13.350] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001791)  
Even our ruffle of cases is open to the public and it is aired via internet.

**[01:55:22.650] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001792)  
Our hearings in the Sandigan Bayan are also made accessible.

**[01:55:27.430] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001793)  
People can listen to the actual hearings conducted

**[01:55:33.910] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001794)  
so that the people can judge for themselves whether we are biased or not.

**[01:55:42.390] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001795)  
So these are our steps to make sure that we are transparent in what we do.

**[01:55:53.860] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001796)  
Internally, we have also made sure that we do not go beyond the period set

**[01:56:00.160] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001797)  
by the Supreme Court to render decisions.

**[01:56:05.280] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001798)  
And that's why I can assure you, Mr. President, Senate President,

**[01:56:10.420] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001799)  
that we are disposing of our cases at the earliest possible time.

**[01:56:19.500] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001800)  
Thank you. Thank you for those explanations, Justice.

**[01:56:21.980] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001801)  
And rest assured that we are one in our objective of making people accountable.

**[01:56:30.160] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001802)  
In fact, that's why I said earlier na meron kaming masusuportahan para bumilis

**[01:56:39.780] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001803)  
at lalong mapanagot natin yung dapat mapanagot.

**[01:56:43.740] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001804)  
Yun ang dapat natin gawin in our own way during the budget deliberations.

**[01:56:48.980] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001805)  
Thank you. And that's why, as mentioned by the Court Administrator,

**[01:56:52.140] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001806)  
the Sandigan Bayan has already moved for its reorganization of its staffing pattern.

**[01:57:00.160] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001807)  
So that we can be more responsive in attending to the cases that are filed.

**[01:57:08.340] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001808)  
At the end of the day, the measure of the people on the performance of the justices

**[01:57:14.140] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001809)  
and the court itself is our disposal of cases at the soonest possible time.

**[01:57:21.080] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001810)  
Thank you for that. And sana mag tuloy-tuloy na itong 82% conviction rate.

**[01:57:27.280] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001811)  
Thank you.

**[01:57:28.300] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001812)  
Thank you.

**[01:57:28.400] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001813)  
Thank you.

**[01:57:30.160] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001814)  
Ang pangarap ni Sen. Pangilinan eh, in many of his speeches.

**[01:57:38.140] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith Econg** (asr-001815)  
When the evidence points to that, po.

**[01:57:40.960] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001816)  
Yes.

**[01:57:42.220] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001817)  
Mabalik lang ako sa admin matters, Justice.

**[01:57:45.140] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001818)  
Yes, go ahead.

**[01:57:47.100] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001819)  
With the permission of our Senate President.

**[01:57:51.280] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001820)  
You know, we were going through some of the conviction rates of other countries

**[01:57:55.740] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001821)  
in terms of corruption cases.

**[01:57:57.180] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001822)

**[01:58:00.160] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001823)

**[01:58:12.220] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001824)

**[01:58:28.060] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001825)

**[01:58:30.160] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001826)

**[01:58:45.300] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001829)

**[01:58:58.280] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001830)

**[01:58:59.620] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001831)

**[01:59:00.160] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001832)

**[01:59:03.580] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001833)

**[01:59:08.080] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001834)

**[01:59:22.000] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001836)

**[02:01:36.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001892)

**[02:01:37.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001893)

**[02:01:45.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001897)

How can

**[02:02:04.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001906)

**[02:02:10.760] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001909)  
Thank you, Your Honor.

**[02:02:12.420] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001910)  
Yes. Your Honor,

**[02:02:13.840] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001911)  
one thing with adjudication is that

**[02:02:15.840] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001912)

**[02:02:17.860] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001913)

**[02:02:20.060] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001914)

**[02:02:22.020] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001915)  
or acquitted. Cases

**[02:02:23.840] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001916)

**[02:02:26.200] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001917)

**[02:02:28.240] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001918)

**[02:02:29.940] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001919)

**[02:02:31.700] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001920)  
the number of lawyers defending an accused,

**[02:02:33.800] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001921)  
the number of motions filed in court.

**[02:02:35.740] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001922)  
So, Your Honor, our

**[02:02:37.600] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001923)  
performance target may be fixed

**[02:02:39.860] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001924)  
statistically. We can

**[02:02:41.980] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001925)  
study the regression rate

**[02:02:43.720] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001926)  
with off anything

**[02:02:45.640] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001927)  
that causes

**[02:02:47.920] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001928)  
its fluctuation,

**[02:02:49.520] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-001929)  
its ebbing or falling, but

**[02:17:41.220] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002332)  
Assuming, you know, maybe.

**[02:17:43.780] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002333)  
At least for us in the Senate,

**[02:17:46.080] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002334)  
it led to a lot of changes in our budget delivery.

**[02:17:52.160] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002335)

**[02:17:54.180] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002336)  
who made it transparent, live-streamed, and the like.

**[02:17:58.080] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002337)

**[02:18:03.380] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002338)  
And I'd like to think that partly, you know,

**[02:18:08.720] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002339)  
your higher conviction rates now may have been also influenced by

**[02:18:13.900] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002340)

**[02:18:22.160] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002341)  
Okay.

**[02:18:23.160] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002342)  
Now, we go to your structural integrity.

**[02:18:27.300] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002343)

**[02:18:36.440] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002344)  
What do you mean?

**[02:18:37.440] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002345)  
Is there...

**[02:18:38.440] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002346)  
Do you need a budget apart from buying or purchasing or building, rather, a new building?

**[02:18:44.720] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002347)  
Is there an immediate intervention to check on the structural defects and correct it?

**[02:18:51.180] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith A. Econg** (asr-002348)  
There were already studies conducted.

**[02:18:53.340] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith A. Econg** (asr-002349)  
One by a private firm.

**[02:18:57.400] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith A. Econg** (asr-002350)  
And this was confirmed by the DPWH as well.

**[02:19:01.220] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith A. Econg** (asr-002351)  
And they confirmed that there are structural defects, indeed,

**[02:19:04.520] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith A. Econg** (asr-002352)  
in our building that we are using right now.

**[02:19:08.300] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith A. Econg** (asr-002353)  
But to repair this would also entail around 400 million, your honors.

**[02:19:17.000] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith A. Econg** (asr-002354)  
But the problem is, if we repair this, we cannot stop hearing cases.

**[02:19:22.580] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith A. Econg** (asr-002355)  
Where we are now, we cannot stop hearing cases.

**[02:19:23.320] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith A. Econg** (asr-002356)  
Where will we go?

**[02:19:24.920] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002357)  
And that's why you are appealing that the 500 million for the new building be...

**[02:19:31.040] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002358)  
Is that right?

**[02:19:31.500] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002359)  
Is that right?

**[02:19:31.860] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002360)  
500 million?

**[02:19:32.940] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith A. Econg** (asr-002361)  
So, we decided we will take the risk, continue occupying a building that is not structurally sound,

**[02:19:44.460] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith A. Econg** (asr-002362)  
and instead opt to build a new building and transfer.

**[02:19:48.360] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith A. Econg** (asr-002363)  
Because we cannot stop the...

**[02:19:52.360] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith A. Econg** (asr-002364)  
Trial.

**[02:19:53.460] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002365)  
But in the meantime, while the construction is ongoing, you remain structurally unsound

**[02:20:00.940] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002366)

**[02:20:03.220] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith A. Econg** (asr-002367)  
Yes, sir.

**[02:20:05.160] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith A. Econg** (asr-002368)  
Ourselves, our staff, and the court users.

**[02:20:09.260] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002369)  
Exactly.

**[02:20:12.260] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002370)

**[02:20:18.620] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002371)  
It's being built.

**[02:20:22.360] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002372)

**[02:20:31.300] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002373)  
We transfer.

**[02:20:32.520] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002374)

**[02:20:38.460] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith A. Econg** (asr-002375)  
Not for now, sir.

**[02:20:40.200] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith A. Econg** (asr-002376)

**[02:20:43.660] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith A. Econg** (asr-002377)  
And we need to fit out the rented space.

**[02:20:47.140] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith A. Econg** (asr-002378)

**[02:20:48.720] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith A. Econg** (asr-002379)  
And when we do, somehow or definitely...

**[02:20:52.360] Sandiganbayan Presiding Justice Geraldine Faith A. Econg** (asr-002380)  
The processing of the cases will also be affected.

**[02:20:58.020] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002381)  
Yeah.

**[02:20:58.540] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002382)  
But, of course, safety first, right?

**[02:21:00.420] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002383)  
That's what they said.

**[02:21:01.300] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002384)

**[02:21:10.220] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002385)

**[02:21:18.500] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002386)  
because we want to make sure that you are safe.

**[02:21:22.360] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002387)

**[02:21:49.950] Sandiganbayan Associate Justice Michael Frederick L. Musngi** (asr-002391)

**[02:21:56.910] Sandiganbayan Associate Justice Michael Frederick L. Musngi** (asr-002392)

**[02:22:02.650] Sandiganbayan Associate Justice Michael Frederick L. Musngi** (asr-002393)

**[02:22:04.730] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002396)

**[02:30:11.740] DBM Merika Joanna S. Dela Peña-Pangan** (asr-002546)

**[02:30:19.860] DBM Merika Joanna S. Dela Peña-Pangan** (asr-002547)

**[02:30:25.300] DBM Merika Joanna S. Dela Peña-Pangan** (asr-002548)

**[02:30:28.540] DBM Merika Joanna S. Dela Peña-Pangan** (asr-002549)  
but we acknowledge that it's in the IRR.

**[02:30:35.340] DBM Merika Joanna S. Dela Peña-Pangan** (asr-002550)  
Nevertheless, if...

**[02:30:37.160] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002551)

**[02:30:38.640] DBM Merika Joanna S. Dela Peña-Pangan** (asr-002552)  
Yes, we agree.

**[02:30:41.720] DBM Merika Joanna S. Dela Peña-Pangan** (asr-002553)

**[02:30:53.520] DBM Merika Joanna S. Dela Peña-Pangan** (asr-002554)  
So we were advised, we asked them if they can secure a copy.

**[02:30:58.540] DBM Merika Joanna S. Dela Peña-Pangan** (asr-002555)  
So we asked them if they can secure a copy from the offices of the Senate President and the Speaker of the House.

**[02:31:02.400] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002556)

**[02:31:05.760] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002557)  
Well, we're number one.

**[02:31:08.880] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002558)

**[02:31:11.000] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002559)

**[02:31:20.200] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002560)

**[02:31:23.620] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002561)

**[02:31:26.240] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002562)

**[02:31:29.660] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002576)

**[02:31:34.900] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002577)  
Okay.

**[02:31:35.260] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002578)

**[02:31:36.960] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002579)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Chairman.

**[02:31:38.180] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002580)

**[02:31:41.380] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002581)  
Okay.

**[02:31:42.280] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002582)  
Okay.

**[02:31:43.820] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002583)  
Another, another, a few more points.

**[02:31:47.180] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002584)  
On the, on the unfilled positions, how does that now, how do you reconcile this with the

**[02:31:55.620] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002585)  
request for additional appropriations?

**[02:31:58.080] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002586)

**[02:32:03.540] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002587)  
30% of your current staffing pattern is unfilled.

**[02:32:09.520] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002588)  
That's right, right?

**[02:32:13.520] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002589)  
Yes.

**[02:32:14.520] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002590)  
So that's a lot, right?

**[02:32:15.840] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002591)  
Yes.

**[02:32:16.840] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002592)  
If you're, what is the PS?

**[02:32:19.840] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002593)  
So if your PS is 58 billion, and your unfilled positions is 30% of your positions in the

**[02:32:27.400] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002594)  
new positions in your plantilia, so of course this is maybe a rough estimate, so that's

**[02:32:34.800] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002595)  
about 30% of 58 billion, maybe, give or take a few billion, but that's a, that's a huge

**[02:32:42.520] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002596)  
amount.

**[02:32:42.860] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002597)  
That's about 10, 15 billion.

**[02:32:46.760] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002598)  
How do you now, how do we now reconcile billions of PS funding for unfilled positions, which

**[02:32:56.460] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002599)  
becomes savings?

**[02:32:58.300] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002600)  
And the request for augmentation, plus the request for creation of new positions, and

**[02:33:05.460] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002601)  
budgetary support for this.

**[02:33:08.320] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002602)  
We will have to reconcile that.

**[02:33:10.000] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002603)  
Yes, sir.

**[02:33:11.200] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002604)  
You can give us initially your comments, and then maybe you can submit to us also in writing

**[02:33:17.820] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002605)  
how we can defend this.

**[02:33:21.480] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002606)  
Because if your savings are big, maybe we can, of course, maybe we can do it.

**[02:33:26.860] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002607)  
Baka pwedeng doon na kunin yung augmentation.

**[02:33:29.480] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002608)  
Hindi ko alam naman.

**[02:33:30.600] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002609)  
But please, go ahead.

**[02:33:32.100] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002610)  
Yes.

**[02:33:32.820] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002611)  
Your Honor, the unfilled positions may either represent the organized courts or the unorganized courts.

**[02:33:40.400] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002612)  
When it comes to organized, they're already existing.

**[02:33:42.920] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002613)  
So it is probable that we call it unfilled because some of the judge may have been promoted,

**[02:33:50.180] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002614)  
have already resigned, or has already retired or passed away.

**[02:33:54.780] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002615)  
Some of the personnel may have done the same.

**[02:33:58.320] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002616)  
But insofar as the courts who have no judges, we do assign other judges to be acting presiding judges on them

**[02:34:09.060] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002617)  
or to assist for that purpose.

**[02:34:12.420] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002618)  
But that's actually the unfilled position.

**[02:34:19.780] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002619)  
So we would need, likewise, even if they are characterized as unfilled, we would need a budget.

**[02:34:24.780] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002620)  
Because there are personnel insofar as those unfilled but organized courts are concerned.

**[02:34:34.000] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002621)  
When you say unfilled, there's no judge?

**[02:34:35.680] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002622)  
There's no judge.

**[02:34:36.560] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002623)  
So it does not necessarily mean the unfilled position includes the court?

**[02:34:40.720] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002624)  
Yes.

**[02:34:41.220] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002625)  
Not the entire court.

**[02:34:42.540] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002626)  
Not the entire court.

**[02:34:43.380] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002627)  
Yes.

**[02:34:43.800] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002628)  
Okay.

**[02:34:44.540] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002629)  
So when you say 30% unfilled, that refers to the judges?

**[02:34:50.000] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002630)  
The judge or the court personnel.

**[02:34:51.460] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002631)  
Can we have a breakdown?

**[02:34:52.480] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002632)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[02:34:52.980] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002633)  
Which would be court personnel?

**[02:34:54.780] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002634)  
Court personnel and which would be judges?

**[02:34:56.940] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002635)  
But for the judges, what is your vacancy?

**[02:35:01.740] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002636)  
For the judges, we have a temporary vacancy, meaning ito po yung nagaantay ng appointment sa presidente, 319.

**[02:35:10.140] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002637)  
Yung unorganized courts po, 170 or a total of 489.

**[02:35:15.840] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002638)  
Yung unorganized po, ito po yung newly created na nagaantay rin po kami ng NOSCA from the Department of Budget and Management.

**[02:35:22.640] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002639)  
And the 489?

**[02:35:24.780] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002640)  
The 489 would be 30%?

**[02:35:27.120] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002641)  
No.

**[02:35:27.580] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002642)  
No.

**[02:35:30.600] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002643)  
Of the entire 16?

**[02:35:33.320] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002644)  
489 vacancy out of 3,000.

**[02:35:36.540] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002645)  
Ano nga ba yung courts natin?

**[02:35:38.280] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002646)  
3,000 courts?

**[02:35:41.740] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002647)  
Ang total po ng courts namin is...

**[02:35:46.300] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002648)  
Yung kaninang ano po?

**[02:35:48.260] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002649)  
3,000.

**[02:35:50.780] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002650)  
We have a total of 2,885 courts po.

**[02:35:56.860] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002651)  
That does not represent yung warm bodies po.

**[02:36:00.280] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002652)  
So 2,800.

**[02:36:01.520] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002653)  
But there are 489 vacancies.

**[02:36:05.000] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002654)  
I cannot do my math po, but that represents a percentage of...

**[02:36:08.860] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002655)  
2,885 divided by...

**[02:36:14.040] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002656)  
489.

**[02:36:15.940] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002657)  
18% your honor.

**[02:36:17.580] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002658)  
18% only.

**[02:36:20.080] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002659)  
Yes po.

**[02:36:21.080] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002660)  
Kasi po madali naman po talaga silang mapunan, ma-filled up.

**[02:36:24.600] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002661)  
The JBS is very active on it.

**[02:36:26.860] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002662)  
The Promotions Selection Board of the...

**[02:36:29.260] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002663)  
In fact, it's low compared to previous years.

**[02:36:32.600] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002664)  
Yes po.

**[02:36:35.080] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002665)  
I remember when I was with the JBC for that eight-year period,

**[02:36:39.100] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002666)  
we were able to bring it down from 32% to 16%.

**[02:36:42.660] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002667)

**[02:36:46.400] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002668)  
And that meant the JBC was interviewing lawyers,

**[02:36:51.280] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002669)

**[02:36:53.280] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002670)  
To fill that up.

**[02:36:55.120] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002671)  
So now it's...

**[02:36:56.860] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002672)  
18%.

**[02:36:57.460] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002673)  
So that's...

**[02:36:58.520] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002674)  
Maybe we should push the JBC.

**[02:37:02.580] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002675)  
Of course, I'm there.

**[02:37:04.900] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002676)  
To bring it down to single digit.

**[02:37:08.540] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002677)  
Yes your honor.

**[02:37:09.340] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002678)  
That's the goal, your honor.

**[02:37:10.500] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002679)  
Yeah.

**[02:37:10.960] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002680)  
Okay.

**[02:37:11.780] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002681)  
Sige.

**[02:37:12.180] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002682)  
So anyway, if you can submit it in writing so that we can be able to defend it on the floor.

**[02:37:18.820] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002683)  
Should there be any questions?

**[02:37:20.520] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002684)  
Yes, sir.

**[02:37:21.840] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002685)  
I guess...

**[02:37:23.480] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002686)  
Vacancies.

**[02:37:26.750] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002687)  
Okay.

**[02:37:28.030] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002688)  
Oh, there you go.

**[02:37:29.350] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002689)  
Yeah.

**[02:37:30.550] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002690)  
Sige.

**[02:37:31.410] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002691)  
Yung unfilled positions and how do we reconcile it with additional funding for newly...

**[02:37:38.150] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002692)  
For positions to be created.

**[02:37:41.310] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002693)  
Okay.

**[02:37:42.590] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002694)  
Well, I have no other questions.

**[02:37:45.510] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002695)

**[02:37:53.430] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002696)

**[02:37:54.650] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002697)

**[02:37:59.650] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002700)

We will do that, Your Honor, Mr.

Yes.

**[02:38:06.630] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002703)  
Okay.

**[02:38:12.560] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002705)

**[02:38:19.700] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002709)

**[02:38:37.000] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002715)

**[02:38:44.320] Court Administrator Theresa Dolores Gomez-Estoesta** (asr-002717)

**[02:38:49.500] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002719)

**[02:39:13.860] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002726)

**[02:39:17.860] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002727)

This is for the CTA.

**[02:39:23.120] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002729)  
The Law on Anti-Agriculture Economic Sabotage Act.

**[02:39:30.280] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002730)  
That the CTA has primary jurisdiction.

**[02:39:35.800] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002731)  
And the complaint has always been that there is no smuggler.

**[02:39:40.280] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002732)  
Anti-economic sabotage is a non-bailable offense.

**[02:39:49.960] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002733)  
And they have announced billions of confiscations.

**[02:40:00.280] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002734)  
They have 4 billion, 500 million, cigarellos.

**[02:40:05.880] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002735)  
And the non-bailable amount is 10 million.

**[02:40:13.700] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002736)  
So, but interesting enough, despite the confiscation of billions,

**[02:40:21.280] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002737)  
there is no smuggler who is still in prison.

**[02:40:26.100] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002738)  
For the value of 10 million.

**[02:40:28.460] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002739)  
So you have all these public pronouncements.

**[02:40:31.280] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002740)  
Of course, this is...

**[02:40:31.880] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002741)  
The whole, you know, the whole slew of the cooperation of the BOC,

**[02:40:36.880] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002742)  
the cooperation of the DOJ, et cetera, prosecution, et cetera,

**[02:40:41.060] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002743)  
to be able to do this.

**[02:40:43.080] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002744)  
But, you know, it's strange that the public announcements are so...

**[02:40:49.080] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002745)  
Are in the billions, hundreds of millions.

**[02:40:51.840] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002746)  
But when it boils down to prosecution, no one is being jailed.

**[02:40:57.880] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002747)  
But there is no...

**[02:40:58.920] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002748)  
There is no non-bailable...

**[02:41:01.880] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002749)  
Offense where the masterminds, you know, are...

**[02:41:07.860] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002750)  
There are those who have been jailed.

**[02:41:09.180] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002751)  
A bodeguero, something like that.

**[02:41:13.840] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002752)  
But anyway, my concern really is,

**[02:41:17.300] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002753)  
what support do you need to be able to achieve the mandate of the Republic Act,

**[02:41:23.260] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002754)  
of which you have primary jurisdiction?

**[02:41:25.400] CTA Associate Justice Jean Marie A. Bacorro-Villena** (asr-002755)  
Okay, good morning, Your Honors.

**[02:41:27.240] CTA Associate Justice Jean Marie A. Bacorro-Villena** (asr-002756)  
First, Your Honor, I would like to give the picture that we have now in the city,

**[02:41:31.880] CTA Associate Justice Jean Marie A. Bacorro-Villena** (asr-002757)  
the law that we are talking about is Republic Act 12022,

**[02:41:38.100] CTA Associate Justice Jean Marie A. Bacorro-Villena** (asr-002758)  
and this was made effective last October 2025.

**[02:41:42.620] CTA Associate Justice Jean Marie A. Bacorro-Villena** (asr-002759)  
And so far, there are about 16 cases pending hearing before our courts.

**[02:41:47.940] CTA Associate Justice Jean Marie A. Bacorro-Villena** (asr-002760)  
And the first case filed is due for promulgation of the decision this October.

**[02:41:54.780] CTA Associate Justice Jean Marie A. Bacorro-Villena** (asr-002761)  
So I agree that there is yet no conviction because the cases were filed.

**[02:41:59.920] CTA Associate Justice Jean Marie A. Bacorro-Villena** (asr-002762)  
The first case was...

**[02:42:01.880] CTA Associate Justice Jean Marie A. Bacorro-Villena** (asr-002763)  
The first case filed was in March 2026.

**[02:42:05.260] CTA Associate Justice Jean Marie A. Bacorro-Villena** (asr-002764)  
So that case will have its promulgation of the decision this October.

**[02:42:11.680] CTA Associate Justice Jean Marie A. Bacorro-Villena** (asr-002765)  
And as for those...

**[02:42:13.960] CTA Associate Justice Jean Marie A. Bacorro-Villena** (asr-002766)  
This is indeed a non-bailable offense for an amount exceeding 10 million,

**[02:42:19.540] CTA Associate Justice Jean Marie A. Bacorro-Villena** (asr-002767)  
and we have denied petitions for bail,

**[02:42:22.580] CTA Associate Justice Jean Marie A. Bacorro-Villena** (asr-002768)  
and there are presently incarcerated, Your Honor.

**[02:42:26.200] CTA Associate Justice Jean Marie A. Bacorro-Villena** (asr-002769)  
Thank you.

**[02:42:33.530] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002770)  
The driver...

**[02:42:36.550] CTA Associate Justice Jean Marie A. Bacorro-Villena** (asr-002771)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[02:42:38.850] CTA Associate Justice Jean Marie A. Bacorro-Villena** (asr-002772)  
There are indeed drivers and passengers who are also included in the information.

**[02:42:45.910] CTA Associate Justice Jean Marie A. Bacorro-Villena** (asr-002773)  
But in the cases that we have been hearing, at least in our division,

**[02:42:51.030] CTA Associate Justice Jean Marie A. Bacorro-Villena** (asr-002774)  
the supposed importer or the owner are also presently detained.

**[02:42:56.490] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002775)  
Okay, okay.

**[02:42:57.810] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002776)  
I don't want you to go into the merits of the case,

**[02:43:00.130] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002777)  
but it's enough to know that that's the status of the case.

**[02:43:05.110] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002778)  
And we hope there'll be more.

**[02:43:13.160] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002779)  
Oh, yeah.

**[02:43:14.120] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002780)  
And the establishment of your courts in the Visayas and Mindanao,

**[02:43:17.940] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002781)  
is that...

**[02:43:18.620] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002782)  
How is that...

**[02:43:21.060] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002783)  
Under the law, you have...

**[02:43:22.940] CTA Associate Justice Jean Marie A. Bacorro-Villena** (asr-002784)  
Under the law, yes, Senator.

**[02:43:24.960] CTA Associate Justice Jean Marie A. Bacorro-Villena** (asr-002785)  
There is that provision in the law that allows us to create new courts in the Visayas and also in Mindanao.

**[02:43:33.540] CTA Associate Justice Jean Marie A. Bacorro-Villena** (asr-002786)  
But so far, with the number of cases that we have been receiving,

**[02:43:37.800] CTA Associate Justice Jean Marie A. Bacorro-Villena** (asr-002787)  
for our discussion last NBank,

**[02:43:40.100] CTA Associate Justice Jean Marie A. Bacorro-Villena** (asr-002788)  
we are not really pushing it this time because of the minimal number of cases being filed.

**[02:43:48.140] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002789)  
Yeah.

**[02:43:48.660] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002790)  
Well, I just wanted this on record so that to inform also our justices and our judges.

**[02:43:58.000] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002791)  
You know, agriculture smuggling is killing the local farming and fishing sector.

**[02:44:07.800] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002792)  
And our food security is compromised.

**[02:44:10.520] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002793)  
Not just food security, food safety.

**[02:44:13.500] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002794)  
Because what we are hearing is what are meant to be condemned in China are smuggled here,

**[02:44:23.540] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002795)  
washed with formalin, and then brought into the wet markets.

**[02:44:31.920] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002796)  
So this is yung salvage value.

**[02:44:35.400] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002797)  
Bibili ng patapon nang mura,

**[02:44:37.260] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002798)  
tapos

**[02:44:37.780] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002799)  
ibebenta dito nang mataas na presyo.

**[02:44:43.040] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002800)  
So that's not just a health risk.

**[02:44:49.060] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002801)  
It's also undermines our food security.

**[02:44:53.520] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002802)  
Pag walang kalaban-laban yung mga magsasaka natin sa presyo na pinapasok,

**[02:45:00.000] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002803)  
hindi na nga tiyak yung kaligtasan mo, 'di ba? Tapos pinapatay pa yung local na industry. That's why smuggling of agricultural products is something that we really have to go hammer and tongs in addressing.

**[02:45:18.640] CTA Associate Justice Jean Marie A. Bacorro-Villena** (asr-002804)  
I agree, Your Honor.

**[02:45:20.100] CTA Associate Justice Jean Marie A. Bacorro-Villena** (asr-002805)  
And I would like also to share that we have already drafted our own rules insofar as the application of search warrants and seizure orders from these smuggled goods.

**[02:45:31.480] CTA Associate Justice Jean Marie A. Bacorro-Villena** (asr-002806)  
So this was made effective last July 1, so just barely eight months after the promulgation or the enactment of the law.

**[02:45:40.060] CTA Associate Justice Jean Marie A. Bacorro-Villena** (asr-002807)  
So this is already in place to facilitate the easier filing of the search warrants and SOs before the CTA.

**[02:45:47.220] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002808)  
And in our anti-smuggling activity.

**[02:45:50.100] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002809)  
Agri-smuggling hearings in the Committee on Agriculture.

**[02:45:54.120] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002810)  
You know, in the last three years, the biggest, the billion peso importer of rice, you know, who was given the necessary permits.

**[02:46:05.220] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002811)  
Five of them were able to import.

**[02:46:08.700] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002812)  
They were the largest and their addresses were the same street.

**[02:46:15.960] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002813)  
And the corporate secretary of one was a.

**[02:46:20.100] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002814)  
Married to a jeepney driver.

**[02:46:22.440] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002815)  
The corporate secretary of the other one was.

**[02:46:27.120] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002816)  
Was the wife of the jeepney driver.

**[02:46:30.760] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002817)  
You know, it's, you know, it's really.

**[02:46:33.560] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002818)  
And that's why the anti-corruption, that's why 10 million non-bailable is critical in terms of enforcement so that.

**[02:46:48.180] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002819)  
But anyway.

**[02:46:50.100] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002820)  
Having said that, whatever the CTA needs in terms of support, if we can augment your budgets, we will do so.

**[02:46:59.280] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002821)  
So that we can fight smuggling and ensure our food security.

**[02:47:06.060] CTA Associate Justice Jean Marie A. Bacorro-Villena** (asr-002822)  
Thank you, Senator.

**[02:47:07.140] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002823)  
Are there other questions from our.

**[02:47:13.040] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002824)  
Yes.

**[02:47:14.000] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002825)  
Okay.

**[02:47:14.560] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002826)  
So there being no other questions, there's a motion.

**[02:47:16.800] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002827)

**[02:47:20.100] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002828)

**[02:47:28.860] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002829)

**[02:47:33.980] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002830)  
Your Honor.

**[02:47:35.480] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002831)  
Thank you, Mr.

**[02:47:36.440] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002832)  
Moved and seconded.

**[02:47:38.660] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002833)  
There being no further questions, and because of the motion duly seconded, the budget of the Judiciary is deemed submitted to plenary.

**[02:47:48.600] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002834)  
Okay.
